value.bind(client) : value\n    44→  },\n    45→})\n    46→\n    47→/**\n    48→ * Send order confirmation email\n    49→ */\n    50→export async function sendOrderConfirmationEmail(params: {\n    51→  to: string\n    52→  orderId: string\n    53→  orderNumber: string\n    54→  itemCount: number\n    55→  totalCents: number\n    56→  currency: string\n    57→  locale: string\n    58→}) {\n    59→  const { to, orderId, orderNumber, itemCount, totalCents, currency, locale } = params\n    60→\n    61→  const { brandName, brandTagline } = await getBrandConfig()\n    62→  const totalAmount = (totalCents / 100).toFixed(2)\n    63→  const currencyCode = currency.toUpperCase()\n    64→\n    65→  // Locale-aware email content\n    66→  const subjects = {\n    67→    en: `Order Confirmation #${orderNumber}`,\n    68→    es: `Confirmación de Pedido #${orderNumber}`,\n    69→    de: `Bestellbestätigung #${orderNumber}`,\n    70→  }\n    71→\n    72→  const headings = {\n    73→    en: 'Thank you for your order! 🎉',\n    74→    es: '¡Gracias por tu pedido! 🎉',\n    75→    de: 'Vielen Dank für deine Bestellung! 🎉',\n    76→  }\n    77→\n    78→  const bodies = {\n    79→    en: `Your order has been confirmed and is being processed. 'item' : 'items',\n    92→    es: itemCount === 1 ? 'artículo' : 'artículos',\n    93→    de: itemCount === 1 ? 'Artikel' : 'Artikel',\n    94→  }\n    95→\n    96→  const totalTexts = {\n    97→    en: 'Total',\n    98→    es: 'Total',\n    99→    de: 'Gesamt',\n   100→  }\n   101→\n   102→  const footerTexts = {\n   103→    en: 'We will send you a shipping confirmation email with tracking information as soon as your order ships.',\n   104→    es: 'Te enviaremos un correo de confirmación de envío con información de rastreo tan pronto como tu pedido sea enviado.',\n   105→    de: 'Wir senden dir eine Versandbestätigungs-E-Mail mit Tracking-Informationen, sobald deine Bestellung versandt wird.',\n   106→  }\n   107→\n   108→  const subject = subjects[locale as keyof typeof subjects] || subjects.en\n   109→  const heading = headings[locale as keyof typeof headings] || headings.en\n   110→  const body = bodies[locale as keyof typeof bodies] || bodies.en\n   111→  const orderSummaryText = orderSummaryTexts[locale as keyof typeof orderSummaryTexts] || orderSummaryTexts.en\n   112→  const itemText = itemTexts[locale as keyof typeof itemTexts] || itemTexts.en\n   113→  const totalText = totalTexts[locale as keyof typeof totalTexts] || totalTexts.en\n   114→  const footerText = footerTexts[locale as keyof typeof footerTexts] || footerTexts.en\n   115→\n   116→  try {\n   117→    const { data, error } = await resend.emails.send({\n   118→      from: `${brandName} <${process.env.RESEND_FROM_EMAIL || 'onboarding@resend.dev'}>`,\n   119→      to,\n   120→      subject,\n   121→      html: `\n   122→<!DOCTYPE html>\n   123→<html>\n   124→<head>\n   125→  <meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n   126→  <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0\">\n   127→  <title>${subject}</title>\n   128→</head>\n   129→<body style=\"font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, 'Helvetica Neue', Arial, sans-serif;     1→/**\n     2→ * Stripe Webhook Handler\n     3→ *\n     4→ * POST /api/webhooks/stripe\n     5→ * Handles Stripe webhook events (checkout.session.completed, etc.)\n     6→ */\n     7→\n     8→import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\n     9→import { headers } from 'next/headers'\n    10→import { stripe } from '@/lib/stripe'\n    11→import { getProvider, initializeProviders } from '@/lib/pod'\n    12→import { canonicalAddressFromStripe } from '@/lib/pod/printify/mapper'\n    13→import { sendOrderConfirmationEmail, sendCreditPurchaseEmail } from '@/lib/resend'\n    14→import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\n    15→import { triggerDripSequence } from '@/lib/email-drip'\n    16→import { BASE_URL } from '@/lib/store-config'\n    17→import Stripe from 'stripe'\n    18→\n    19→// Initialize Supabase client with service role key for webhook\n    20→const supabase = createClient(\n    21→  process.env.SUPABASE_URL!,\n    22→  process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY!\n    23→)\n    24→\n    25→export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n    26→  const body = await req.text()\n    27→  const headersList = await headers()\n    28→  const signature = headersList.get('stripe-signature')\n    29→\n    30→  if (!signature) {\n    31→    return NextResponse.json(\n    32→      { error: 'Missing stripe-signature header' },\n    33→      { status: 400 }\n    34→    )\n    35→  }\n    36→\n    37→  let event: Stripe.Event\n    38→\n    39→  try {\n    40→    // Verify webhook signature\n    41→    event = stripe.webhooks.constructEvent(\n    42→      body,\n    43→      signature,\n    44→      process.env.STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET!\n    45→    )\n    46→  } catch (err) {\n    47→    console.error('Webhook signature verification failed:', err)\n    48→    return NextResponse.json(\n    49→      { error: 'Webhook signature verification failed' },\n    50→      { status: 400 }\n    51→    )\n    52→  }\n    53→\n    54→  // Handle the event\n    55→  switch (event.type) {\n    56→    case 'checkout.session.completed':\n    57→      await handleCheckoutSessionCompleted(event.data.object as Stripe.Checkout.Session)\n    58→      break\n    59→\n    60→    case 'customer.subscription.created':\n    61→    case 'customer.subscription.updated':\n    62→      await handleSubscriptionUpdate(event.data.object as Stripe.Subscription)\n    63→      break\n    64→\n    65→    case 'customer.subscription.deleted':\n    66→      await handleSubscriptionDeleted(event.data.object as Stripe.Subscription)\n    67→      break\n    68→\n    69→    case 'payment_intent.succeeded':\n    70→      console.log('PaymentIntent succeeded:', event.data.object.id)\n    71→      break\n    72→\n    73→    case 'payment_intent.payment_failed':\n    74→      console.log('PaymentIntent failed:', event.data.object.id)\n    75→      break\n    76→\n    77→    case 'invoice.payment_failed':\n    78→      await handleInvoicePaymentFailed(event.data.object as Stripe.Invoice)\n    79→      break\n    80→\n    81→    case 'charge.dispute.created':\n    82→      await handleChargeDisputeCreated(event.data.object as Stripe.Dispute)\n    83→      break\n    84→\n    85→    default:\n    86→      console.log(`Unhandled event type: ${event.type}`)\n    87→  }\n    88→\n    89→  return NextResponse.json({ received: true })\n    90→}\n    91→\n    92→/**\n    93→ * Handle checkout.session.completed event\n    94→ * Creates an order in the database when payment is successful\n    95→ */\n    96→async function handleCheckoutSessionCompleted(session: Stripe.Checkout.Session) {\n    97→  try {\n    98→    console.log('Processing checkout.session.completed:', session.id)\n    99→\n   100→    // Only process paid sessions\n   101→    if (session.payment_status !== 'paid') {\n   102→      console.log('Session not paid yet:', session.id)\n   103→      return\n   104→    }\n   105→\n   106→    // Extract metadata\n   107→    const locale = session.metadata?.locale || 'en'\n   108→    const cartItemsStr = session.metadata?.cart_items || '[]'\n   109→    const cartItems = JSON.parse(cartItemsStr)\n   110→    const giftMessage = session.metadata?.gift_message || null\n   111→\n   112→    // Get session details with line items and payment method\n   113→    const fullSession = await stripe.checkout.sessions.retrieve(session.id, {\n   114→      expand: ['line_items', 'payment_intent', 'payment_intent.payment_method'],\n   115→    })\n   116→\n   117→    const lineItems = fullSession.line_items?.data || []\n   118→\n   119→    // Get customer email\n   120→    const customerEmail = session.customer_details?.email || session.customer_email\n   121→\n   122→    // Build shipping address JSONB\n   123→    const shipping = session.collected_information?.shipping_details\n   124→    const shippingAddress = shipping?.address\n   125→      ? {\n   126→          name: shipping.name,\n   127→          line1: shipping.address.line1,\n   128→          line2: shipping.address.line2,\n   129→          city: shipping.address.city,\n   130→          state: shipping.address.state,\n   131→          postal_code: shipping.address.postal_code,\n   132→          country: shipping.address.country,\n   133→        }\n   134→      : null\n   135→\n   136→    // Get payment intent ID and payment method\n   137→    const paymentIntentId =\n   138→      typeof fullSession.payment_intent === 'string'\n   139→        ? fullSession.payment_intent\n   140→        : fullSession.payment_intent?.id\n   141→\n   142→    // Get payment method type (card, crypto, etc.)\n   143→    let paymentMethodType: string | null = null\n   144→    if (typeof fullSession.payment_intent !== 'string' && fullSession.payment_intent?.payment_method) {\n   145→      const paymentMethod = fullSession.payment_intent.payment_method\n   146→      if (typeof paymentMethod !== 'string') {\n   147→        paymentMethodType = paymentMethod.type || null\n   148→      }\n   149→    }\n   150→\n   151→    // Idempotency check: Check if order already exists for this session\n   152→    const { data: existingOrder } = await supabase\n   153→      .from('orders')\n   154→      .select('id, status, created_at')\n   155→      .eq('stripe_session_id', session.id)\n   156→      .single()\n   157→\n   158→    if (existingOrder) {\n   159→      console.log('Order already exists for session:', session.id, '— skipping (idempotent)')\n   160→      console.log('Existing order ID:', existingOrder.id)\n   161→      return // Idempotent: order already processed\n   162→    }\n   163→\n   164→    // Look up user by email (for authenticated users)\n   165→    let userId: string | null = null\n   166→    if (customerEmail) {\n   167→      const { data: userByEmail } = await supabase\n   168→        .from('users')\n   169→        .select('id')\n   170→        .eq('email', customerEmail)\n   171→        .single()\n   172→      userId = userByEmail?.id || null\n   173→    }\n   174→\n   175→    // Create order record\n   176→    const { data: order, error: orderError } = await supabase\n   177→      .from('orders')\n   178→      .insert({\n   179→        user_id: userId,\n   180→        stripe_session_id: session.id,\n   181→        stripe_payment_intent_id: paymentIntentId,\n   182→        status: 'paid',\n   183→        total_cents: session.amount_total || 0,\n   184→        currency: session.currency || 'eur',\n   185→        shipping_address: shippingAddress,\n   186→        customer_email: customerEmail,\n   187→        locale,\n   188→        gift_message: giftMessage,\n   189→        payment_method: paymentMethodType,\n   190→        paid_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n   191→      })\n   192→      .select()\n   193→      .single()\n   194→\n   195→    if (orderError) {\n   196→      console.error('Failed to create order:', orderError)\n   197→      throw orderError\n   198→    }\n   199→\n   200→    console.log('Created order:', order.id)\n   201→\n   202→    // Create order items (variant_id is required — NOT NULL constraint)\n   203→    const orderItems = lineItems.map((item, index) => {\n   204→      const cartItem = cartItems[index] || {}\n   205→      return {\n   206→        order_id: order.id,\n   207→        product_id: cartItem.product_id || null,\n   208→        variant_id: cartItem.variant_id,\n   209→        personalization_id: cartItem.personalization_id || null,\n   210→        composition_id: cartItem.composition_id || null,\n   211→        quantity: item.quantity || 1,\n   212→        unit_price_cents: Math.round((item.amount_total || 0) / (item.quantity || 1)),\n   213→      }\n   214→    })\n   215→\n   216→    // Filter out items without product_id or variant_id (data integrity check)\n   217→    const validOrderItems = orderItems.filter((item) => item.product_id && item.variant_id)\n   218→\n   219→    if (validOrderItems.length > 0) {\n   220→      const { error: itemsError } = await supabase\n   221→        .from('order_items')\n   222→        .insert(validOrderItems)\n   223→\n   224→      if (itemsError) {\n   225→        console.error('Failed to create order items:', itemsError)\n   226→        // Don't throw - order is already created\n   227→      } else {\n   228→        console.log(`Created ${validOrderItems.length} order items`)\n   229→      }\n   230→    }\n   231→\n   232→    // Create notification for the user (if user_id is found)\n   233→    // For guest checkouts, we'll create a notification linked to the email\n   234→    // Note: In a real system, you'd look up user by email first\n   235→    const orderDisplayId = order.id.slice(0, 8) // Use first 8 chars of UUID as display ID\n   236→    const itemCount = validOrderItems.length\n   237→    const totalAmount = (order.total_cents / 100).toFixed(2)\n   238→    const currencyCode = order.currency.toUpperCase()\n   239→\n   240→    const notificationData = {\n   241→      type: 'order_confirmation',\n   242→      title: `Order #${orderDisplayId} Confirmed`,\n   243→      body: `Your order has been confirmed. ${itemCount} ${itemCount === 1 ? 'item' : 'items'} • Total: ${totalAmount} ${currencyCode}`,\n   244→      data: {\n   245→        order_id: order.id,\n   246→        session_id: session.id,\n   247→        total_cents: order.total_cents,\n   248→        currency: order.currency,\n   249→        item_count: itemCount,\n   250→      },\n   251→      is_read: false,\n   252→    }\n   253→\n   254→    // Create notification for authenticated user (reuse userId from earlier lookup)\n   255→    if (userId) {\n   256→      const { error: notifError } = await supabase\n   257→        .from('notifications')\n create notification:', notifError)\n   265→      } else {\n   266→        console.log('Created notification for user:', userId)\n   267→      }\n   268→    } else {\n   269→      console.log('No user found for email - skipping notification (guest checkout)')\n   270→    }\n   271→\n   272→    // Create audit log entry\n   273→    const { error: auditError } = await supabase\n   274→      .from('audit_log')\n   275→      .insert({\n   276→        actor_type: 'webhook',\n   277→        actor_id: 'stripe_webhook',\n   278→        action: 'order_created',\n   279→        resource_type: 'order',\n   280→        resource_id: order.id,\n   281→        changes: {\n   282→          status: 'paid',\n   283→          total_cents: order.total_cents,\n   284→          currency: order.currency,\n   285→        },\n   286→        metadata: {\n   287→          stripe_session_id: session.id,\n   288→          stripe_payment_intent_id: paymentIntentId,\n   289→          customer_email: customerEmail,\n   290→          locale,\n   291→        },\n   292→      })\n   293→\n   294→    if (auditError) {\n   295→      console.error('Failed to create audit log entry:', auditError)\n   296→    } else {\n   297→      console.log('Created audit log entry for order:', order.id)\n   298→    }\n   299→\n   300→    // Increment coupon usage counter if a coupon was applied\n   301→    const couponCode = session.metadata?.coupon_code\n   302→    if (couponCode) {\n   303→      const { data: coupon } = await supabase\n   304→        .from('coupons')\n   305→        .select('id, times_used')\n   306→        .eq('code', couponCode)\n   307→        .single()\n   308→\n   309→      if (coupon) {\n   310→        const { error: couponError } = await supabase\n   311→          .from('coupons')\n   312→          .update({ times_used: (coupon.times_used || 0) + 1 })\n   313→          .eq('id', coupon.id)\n   314→\n   315→        if (couponError) {\n   316→          console.error('Failed to increment coupon usage:', couponError)\n   317→        } else {\n   318→          console.log(`Incremented coupon \"${couponCode}\" usage to ${(coupon.times_used || 0) + 1}`)\n   319→        }\n   320→      }\n   321→    }\n   322→\n   323→    // Submit order to POD provider\n   324→    if (validOrderItems.length > 0 && shippingAddress && customerEmail) {\n   325→      try {\n   326→        initializeProviders()\n   327→        const provider = getProvider()\n   328→        console.log('Submitting order to POD provider...')\n   329→\n   330→        // Fetch product and variant provider IDs\n   331→        const productIds = validOrderItems.map(item => item.product_id)\n   332→        const variantIds = validOrderItems.map(item => item.variant_id).filter(Boolean)\n   333→\n   334→        const { data: products } = await supabase\n   335→          .from('products')\n   336→          .select('id, provider_product_id')\n   337→          .in('id', productIds)\n   338→\n   339→        const { data: variants } = await supabase\n   340→          .from('product_variants')\n   341→          .select('id, external_variant_id')\n   342→          .in('id', variantIds)\n   343→\n   344→        // Create lookup maps\n   345→        const productMap = new Map(products?.map(p => [p.id, p.provider_product_id]) || [])\n   346→        const variantMap = new Map(variants?.map(v => [v.id, v.external_variant_id]) || [])\n   347→\n   348→        // Guard: verify all items have valid provider variant mappings\n   349→        const itemsMissingProviderVariant = validOrderItems.filter(item => {\n   350→          const pvId = variantMap.get(item.variant_id)\n   351→          return !pvId || pvId === '0'\n   352→        })\n   353→\n   354→        if (itemsMissingProviderVariant.length > 0) {\n   355→          console.error('Order items missing provider variant mapping:', itemsMissingProviderVariant)\n   356→          await supabase.from('orders').update({\n   357→            pod_error: 'Items missing provider variant mapping',\n   358→            status: 'requires_review',\n   359→          }).eq('id', order.id)\n   360→\n   361→          await notifyAdminOfProviderFailure(order.id, 'variant_mapping', 'One or more items have no provider variant mapping')\n   362→          if (customerEmail) {\n   363→            await sendOrderIssueEmail(customerEmail, order.id, order.locale || 'en')\n   364→          }\n   365→          return\n   366→        }\n   367→\n   368→        // Build production URLs map from cart metadata (keyed by composition_id)\n   369→        const productionUrlsMap = new Map<string, Record<string, string>>()\n   370→        for (const ci of cartItems) {\n   371→          if (ci.composition_id && ci.production_urls) {\n   372→            productionUrlsMap.set(ci.composition_id, ci.production_urls)\n   373→          }\n   374→        }\n   375→\n   376→        // Build provider line items (variant_id is guaranteed non-null)\n   377→        const providerLineItems = validOrderItems\n   378→          .filter(item => {\n   379→            const providerProductId = productMap.get(item.product_id)\n   380→            const providerVariantId = variantMap.get(item.variant_id)\n   381→            return providerProductId && providerVariantId\n   382→          })\n   383→          .map(item => {\n   384→            const base = {\n   385→              product_id: productMap.get(item.product_id)!,\n   386→              variant_id: parseInt(variantMap.get(item.variant_id)!, 10),\n   387→              quantity: item.quantity,\n   388→            }\n   389→            // If custom design, include production file URLs\n   390→            const prodUrls = item.composition_id ? productionUrlsMap.get(item.composition_id) : null\n   391→            if (prodUrls) {\n   392→              return {\n   393→                ...base,\n   394→                files: Object.entries(prodUrls).map(([placement, url]) => ({\n   395→                  type: placement,\n   396→                  url: url as string,\n   397→                })),\n   398→              }\n   399→            }\n   400→            return base\n   401→          })\n   402→\n   403→        if (providerLineItems.length === 0) {\n   404→          console.log('No valid provider line items - skipping POD submission')\n   405→        } else {\n   406→          // Create POD provider order\n   407→          const canonicalAddress = canonicalAddressFromStripe(shippingAddress, customerEmail)\n   408→\n   409→          const printifyOrder = await provider.createOrder({\n   410→            internalOrderId: order.id,\n   411→            label: `Order ${order.id.slice(0, 8)}`,\n   412→            lineItems: providerLineItems.map((li) => ({\n   413→              productExternalId: li.product_id,\n   414→              variantExternalId: String(li.variant_id),\n   415→              quantity: li.quantity,\n   416→              ...('files' in li && li.files ? { files: li.files } : {}),\n   417→            })),\n   418→            shippingAddress: canonicalAddress,\n   419→            suppressShippingNotification: false,\n   420→          })\n   421→\n   422→          console.log('Created POD provider order:', printifyOrder.externalId)\n   423→\n   424→          // Update order with provider order ID\n   425→          const { error: updateError } = await supabase\n   426→            .from('orders')\n   427→            .update({\n   428→              external_order_id: printifyOrder.externalId,\n   429→              pod_provider: 'printful',\n   430→              status: 'submitted', // Update status to submitted\n   431→              pod_last_attempt_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n   432→            })\n   433→            .eq('id', order.id)\n   434→\n   435→          if (updateError) {\n   436→            console.error('Failed to update order with provider order ID:', updateError)\n   437→          } else {\n   438→            console.log('Updated order with provider order ID')\n   439→          }\n   440→\n   441→          // Submit order for production\n   442→          try {\n   443→            await provider.submitForProduction(printifyOrder.externalId)\n   444→            console.log('Submitted POD order for production')\n   445→          } catch (productionError) {\n   446→            console.error('Failed to submit POD order for production:', productionError)\n   447→\n   448→            // Mark order for retry\n   449→            const errorMessage = productionError instanceof Error\n   450→              ? productionError.message\n   451→              : 'Failed to submit order for production'\n   452→\n   453→            await supabase\n   454→              .from('orders')\n   455→              .update({\n   456→                pod_error: errorMessage,\n   457→                pod_retry_count: 1,\n   458→                pod_last_attempt_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n   459→              })\n   460→              .eq('id', order.id)\n   461→\n   462→            // Notify admin and customer of production failure\n   463→            await notifyAdminOfProviderFailure(order.id, 'production', errorMessage)\n   464→            if (customerEmail) {\n   465→              await sendOrderIssueEmail(customerEmail, order.id, order.locale || 'en')\n   466→            }\n   467→          }\n   468→        }\n   469→      } catch (printifyError) {\n   470→        console.error('Error submitting order to POD provider:', printifyError)\n   471→\n   472→        // Mark order for retry with error details\n   473→        const errorMessage = printifyError instanceof Error\n   474→          ? printifyError.message\n   475→          : 'Unknown POD provider error'\n   476→\n   477→        await supabase\n   478→          .from('orders')\n   479→          .update({\n   480→            pod_error: errorMessage,\n   481→            pod_retry_count: 1,\n   482→            pod_last_attempt_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n   483→          })\n   484→          .eq('id', order.id)\n   485→\n   486→        // Notify admin and customer of the failure\n   487→        await notifyAdminOfProviderFailure(order.id, 'submission', errorMessage)\n   488→        if (customerEmail) {\n   489→          await sendOrderIssueEmail(customerEmail, order.id, order.locale || 'en')\n   490→        }\n   491→\n   492→        // Don't throw - we don't want to fail the entire webhook\n   493→        // The order is still created in our system and marked for retry\n   494→      }\n   495→    } else {\n   496→      console.log('Missing shipping address or items - skipping POD submission')\n   497→    }\n   498→\n   499→    // Handle credit pack purchases\n   500→    if (session.metadata?.type === 'credit_pack' && session.metadata?.user_id) {\n   501→      await handleCreditPackPurchase(session)\n   502→    }\n   503→\n   504→    // Send order confirmation email\n   505→    if (customerEmail) {\n   506→      try {\n   507→        const orderNumber = order.id.slice(0, 8)\n   508→        await sendOrderConfirmationEmail({\n   509→          to: customerEmail,\n   510→          orderId: order.id,\n   511→          orderNumber,\n   512→          itemCount: validOrderItems.length,\n   513→          totalCents: order.total_cents,\n   514→          currency: order.currency,\n   515→          locale: order.locale || 'en',\n   516→        })\n   517→        console.log('Order confirmation email sent to:', customerEmail)\n   518→      } catch (emailError) {\n   519→        console.error('Failed to send order confirmation email:', emailError)\n   520→        // Don't throw - email failure shouldn't fail the webhook\n   521→      }\n   522→    }\n   523→\n   524→    console.log('Successfully processed checkout session:', session.id)\n   525→  } catch (error) {\n   526→    console.error('Error handling checkout session:', error)\n   527→    // Don't throw - we don't want to cause Stripe to retry indefinitely\n   528→  }\n   529→}\n   530→\n   531→/**\n   532→ * Handle subscription created/updated\n   533→ * Updates user tier and subscription info\n   534→ */\n   535→async function handleSubscriptionUpdate(subscription: Stripe.Subscription) {\n   536→  try {\n   537→    // Find user by Stripe customer ID\n   538→    const customerId = typeof subscription.customer === 'string'\n   539→      ? subscription.customer\n   540→      : subscription.customer.id\n   541→\n   542→    const { data: user, error: userError } = await supabase\n   543→      .from('users')\n   544→      .select('id, credit_balance')\n   545→      .eq('stripe_customer_id', customerId)\n   546→      .single()\n   547→\n   548→    if (userError || !user) {\n   549→      console.error('Subscription update: user not found for customer', customerId)\n   550→      return\n   551→    }\n   552→\n   553→    const isActive = subscription.status === 'active'\n   554→    const periodEnd = (subscription as any).current_period_end\n   555→      ? new Date((subscription as any).current_period_end * 1000).toISOString()\n   556→      : null\n   557→\n   558→    // Update user tier and subscription info\n   559→    const { error: updateError } = await supabase\n   560→      .from('users')\n   561→      .update({\n   562→        tier: isActive ? 'premium' : 'free',\n   563→        stripe_subscription_id: subscription.id,\n   564→        subscription_status: subscription.status === 'active' ? 'active'\n   565→          : subscription.status === 'past_due' ? 'past_due'\n   566→          : 'none',\n   567→        subscription_period_end: periodEnd,\n   568→      })\n   569→      .eq('id', user.id)\n   570→\n   571→    if (updateError) {\n   572→      console.error('Failed to update user subscription:', updateError)\n   573→      return\n   574→    }\n   575→\n   576→    // Add monthly bonus credits on new subscription activation (atomic)\n   577→    if (isActive) {\n   578→      const bonusCredits = 10\n   579→\n   580→      const { data: bonusResult } = await supabase.rpc('add_credits', {\n   581→        p_user_id: user.id,\n   582→        p_amount: bonusCredits,\n   583→      })\n   584→\n   585→      const newBalance = bonusResult?.balance ?? 0\n   586→\n   587→      await supabase.from('credit_transactions').insert({\n   588→        user_id: user.id,\n   589→        amount: bonusCredits,\n   590→        reason: 'subscription_bonus',\n   591→        balance_after: newBalance,\n   592→      })\n   593→\n   594→      console.log(`Added ${bonusCredits} bonus credits for user ${user.id}`)\n   595→    }\n   596→\n   597→    // Trigger welcome drip sequence for new subscribers\n   598→    if (isActive) {\n   599→      const { data: userProfile } = await supabase\n   600→        .from('users')\n   601→        .select('email')\n   602→        .eq('id', user.id)\n   603→        .single()\n   604→\n   605→      if (userProfile?.email) {\n   606→        triggerDripSequence(user.id, userProfile.email, 'welcome').catch((err) =>\n   607→          console.error('Failed to trigger drip sequence:', err)\n   608→        )\n   609→      }\n   610→    }\n   611→\n   612→    console.log(`Updated subscription for user ${user.id}: tier=${isActive ? 'premium' : 'free'}`)\n   613→  } catch (error) {\n   614→    console.error('Error handling subscription update:', error)\n   615→  }\n   616→}\n   617→\n   618→/**\n   619→ * Handle subscription deleted/cancelled\n   620→ */\n   621→async function handleSubscriptionDeleted(subscription: Stripe.Subscription) {\n   622→  try {\n   623→    const customerId = typeof subscription.customer === 'string'\n   624→      ? subscription.customer\n   625→      : subscription.customer.id\n   626→\n   627→    const { error } = await supabase\n   628→      .from('users')\n   629→      .update({\n   630→        tier: 'free',\n   631→        subscription_status: 'cancelled',\n   632→      })\n   633→      .eq('stripe_customer_id', customerId)\n   634→\n   635→    if (error) {\n   636→      console.error('Failed to handle subscription deletion:', error)\n   637→    } else {\n   638→      console.log('Subscription cancelled for customer:', customerId)\n   639→    }\n   640→  } catch (error) {\n   641→    console.error('Error handling subscription deletion:', error)\n   642→  }\n   643→}\n   644→\n   645→/**\n   646→ * Handle credit pack purchase from checkout.session.completed\n   647→ * Idempotent: uses UNIQUE(user_id, stripe_payment_id) to prevent double-crediting on webhook retries.\n   648→ * Atomic: uses credit_balance = credit_balance + N (no SELECT-then-UPDATE).\n   649→ */\n   650→async function handleCreditPackPurchase(session: Stripe.Checkout.Session) {\n   651→  try {\n   652→    const userId = session.metadata?.user_id\n   653→    const credits = parseInt(session.metadata?.credits || '0')\n   654→    const paymentId = (session.payment_intent as string) || session.id\n   655→\n   656→    if (!userId || !credits || !paymentId) return\n   657→\n   658→    // Idempotency: insert transaction first — UNIQUE(user_id, stripe_payment_id) rejects duplicates\n   659→    // balance_after is set to 0 temporarily, updated after atomic increment\n   660→    const { error: txError } = await supabase.from('credit_transactions').insert({\n   661→      user_id: userId,\n   662→      amount: credits,\n   663→      reason: 'purchase',\n   664→      stripe_payment_id: paymentId,\n   665→      balance_after: 0,\n   666→    })\n   667→\n   668→    if (txError) {\n   669→      // UNIQUE violation = already processed (idempotent)\n   670→      if (txError.code === '23505') {\n   671→        console.log(`Credit pack already processed for payment ${paymentId} — skipping (idempotent)`)\n   672→        return\n   673→      }\n   674→      throw txError\n   675→    }\n   676→\n   677→    // Atomic balance update via RPC: credit_balance = credit_balance + N (no race condition)\n   678→    const { data: rpcResult } = await supabase.rpc('add_credits', {\n   679→      p_user_id: userId,\n   680→      p_amount: credits,\n   681→    })\n   682→\n   683→    const newBalance = rpcResult?.balance ?? 0\n   684→\n   685→    // Backfill the correct balance_after in the transaction record\n   686→    await supabase\n   687→      .from('credit_transactions')\n   688→      .update({ balance_after: newBalance })\n   689→      .eq('user_id', userId)\n   690→      .eq('stripe_payment_id', paymentId)\n   691→\n   692→    console.log(`Added ${credits} credits for user ${userId} (new balance: ${newBalance})`)\n   693→\n   694→    // Fetch user for email\n   695→    const { data: user } = await supabase\n   696→      .from('users')\n   697→      .select('email, locale')\n   698→      .eq('id', userId)\n   699→      .single()\n   700→\n   701→    // Send confirmation email\n   702→    if (user?.email) {\n   703→      try {\n   704→        await sendCreditPurchaseEmail({\n   705→          to: user.email,\n   706→          credits,\n   707→          priceCents: session.amount_total || 0,\n   708→          currency: session.currency || 'eur',\n   709→          newBalance,\n   710→          locale: user.locale || 'en',\n   711→        })\n   712→        console.log(`Credit purchase email sent to: ${user.email}`)\n   713→      } catch (emailError) {\n   714→        console.error('Failed to send credit purchase email:', emailError)\n   715→      }\n   716→    }\n   717→  } catch (error) {\n   718→    console.error('Error handling credit pack purchase:', error)\n   719→  }\n   720→}\n   721→\n   722→/**\n   723→ * Handle invoice.payment_failed event\n   724→ * Updates subscription status and notifies user + admin\n   725→ */\n   726→async function handleInvoicePaymentFailed(invoice: Stripe.Invoice) {\n   727→  try {\n   728→    const customerId = typeof invoice.customer === 'string'\n   729→      ? invoice.customer\n   730→      : invoice.customer?.id\n   731→\n   732→    if (!customerId) return\n   733→\n   734→    // Find user\n   735→    const { data: user } = await supabase\n   736→      .from('users')\n   737→      .select('id, email')\n   738→      .eq('stripe_customer_id', customerId)\n   739→      .single()\n   740→\n   741→    if (!user) {\n   742→      console.warn('Invoice payment failed: no user found for customer', customerId)\n   743→      return\n   744→    }\n   745→\n   746→    // Update subscription status to past_due\n   747→    await supabase\n   748→      .from('users')\n   749→      .update({ subscription_status: 'past_due' })\n   750→      .eq('id', user.id)\n   751→\n   752→    // Send payment failure email via Resend\n   753→    const resendKey = process.env.RESEND_API_KEY\n   754→    if (resendKey && user.email) {\n   755→      await fetch('https://api.resend.com/emails', {\n   756→        method: 'POST',\n   757→        headers: {\n   758→          'Authorization': `Bearer ${resendKey}`,\n   759→          'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n   760→        },\n   761→        body: JSON.stringify({\n   762→          from: process.env.RESEND_FROM_EMAIL || 'SKAPARA <noreply@skapara.com>',\n   763→          to: user.email,\n   764→          subject: 'Payment Failed — Please Update Your Payment Method',\n   765→          html: `\n   766→            <h1>Payment Failed</h1>\n   767→            <p>We were unable to process your subscription payment.</p>\n   768→            <p>Please update your payment method to keep your Premium features active.</p>\n   769→            <p><a href=\"${BASE_URL}/profile\">Update Payment Method →</a></p>\n   770→          `,\n   771→        }),\n   772→      }).catch((err) => console.error('Failed to send payment failure email:', err))\n   773→    }\n   774→\n   775→    // Alert admin\n   776→    fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/admin/alert`, {\n   777→      method: 'POST',\n   778→      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n   779→      body: JSON.stringify({\n   780→        type: 'invoice_payment_failed',\n   781→        message: `Payment failed for user ${user.email || user.id}`,\n   782→        severity: 'medium',\n   783→      }),\n   784→    }).catch(() => {})\n   785→\n   786→    console.log(`Invoice payment failed for user ${user.id}, status set to past_due`)\n   787→  } catch (error) {\n   788→    console.error('Error handling invoice payment failed:', error)\n   789→  }\n   790→}\n   791→\n   792→/**\n   793→ * Notify admin of POD provider submission failure\n   794→ * Creates a notification for all admin users\n   795→ */\n   796→/**\n   797→ * Notify the customer that their order requires manual review.\n   798→ * Uses Resend email service.\n   799→ */\n   800→async function sendOrderIssueEmail(email: string, orderId: string, locale: string) {\n   801→  try {\n   802→    const resendKey = process.env.RESEND_API_KEY\n   803→    if (!resendKey) return\n   804→\n   805→    const orderNumber = orderId.slice(0, 8)\n   806→\n   807→    await fetch('https://api.resend.com/emails', {\n   808→      method: 'POST',\n   809→      headers: {\n   810→        'Authorization': `Bearer ${resendKey}`,\n   811→        'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n   812→      },\n   813→      body: JSON.stringify({\n   814→        from: process.env.RESEND_FROM_EMAIL || 'SKAPARA <noreply@skapara.com>',\n   815→        to: — Überprüfung erforderlich`\n   820→            : `Order #${orderNumber} — Review Required`,\n   821→        html: locale === 'es'\n   822→          ? `<h1>Tu pedido requiere revisión</h1><p>Estamos to ${email} for order ${orderNumber}`)\n   829→  } catch (err) {\n   830→    console.error('Failed to send order issue email:', err)\n   831→  }\n   832→}\n   833→\n   834→async function notifyAdminOfProviderFailure(\n   835→  orderId: string,\n   836→  failureType: 'submission' | 'production' | 'variant_mapping',\n   837→  errorMessage: string\n   838→) {\n   839→  try {\n   840→    // Find all admin users\n   841→    const { data: admins } = await supabase\n   842→      .from('users')\n   843→      .select('id')\n   844→      .eq('role', 'admin')\n   845→\n   846→    if (!admins || admins.length === 0) {\n   847→      console.warn('No admin users found - cannot send provider failure notification')\n   848→      return\n   849→    }\n   850→\n   851→    // Create notification for each admin\n   852→    const notifications = admins.map(admin => ({\n   853→      user_id: admin.id,\n   854→      type: 'pod_error',\n   855→      title: `Provider ${failureType} failed`,\n   856→      body: `Order ${orderId.slice(0, 8)} failed to submit to provider: ${errorMessage}`,\n   857→      data: {\n   858→        order_id: orderId,\n   859→        failure_type: failureType,\n   860→        error: errorMessage,\n   861→      },\n   862→      is_read: false,\n   863→    }))\n   864→\n   865→    const { error } = await supabase\n   866→      .from('notifications')\n   867→      .insert(notifications)\n   868→\n   869→    if (error) {\n   870→      console.error('Failed to create admin notifications:', error)\n   871→    } else {\n   872→      console.log(`Created ${notifications.length} admin notifications for provider failure`)\n   873→    }\n   874→  } catch (error) {\n   875→    console.error('Error notifying admin of provider failure:', error)\n   876→  }\n   877→}\n   878→\n   879→/**\n   880→ * Handle charge.dispute.created event\n   881→ * Marks order as disputed, alerts admin, and pauses fulfillment\n   882→ */\n   883→async function handleChargeDisputeCreated(dispute: Stripe.Dispute) {\n   884→  try {\n   885→    console.log('Processing charge.dispute.created:', dispute.id)\n   886→\n   887→    // Get the payment intent ID from the dispute\n   888→    const paymentIntentId = typeof dispute.payment_intent === 'string'\n   889→      ? dispute.payment_intent\n   890→      : dispute.payment_intent?.id\n   891→\n   892→    if (!paymentIntentId) {\n   893→      console.warn('No payment intent ID found in dispute:', dispute.id)\n   894→      return\n   895→    }\n   896→\n   897→    // Find the order by payment intent ID\n   898→    const { data: order, error: orderError } = await supabase\n   899→      .from('orders')\n   900→      .select('id, customer_email, total_cents, currency, status')\n   901→      .eq('stripe_payment_intent_id', paymentIntentId)\n   902→      .single()\n   903→\n   904→    if (orderError || !order) {\n   905→      console.warn('Dispute: order not found for payment intent:', paymentIntentId)\n   906→      return\n   907→    }\n   908→\n   909→    // Update order status to 'cancelled' to pause fulfillment\n   910→    // TODO: Add 'disputed' status to orders table CHECK constraint and add columns:\n   911→    // stripe_dispute_id, stripe_dispute_reason, stripe_dispute_amount\n   912→    const { error: updateError } = await supabase\n   913→      .from('orders')\n   914→      .update({\n   915→        status: 'cancelled', // Using existing status until migration adds 'disputed'\n   916→        updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n   917→      })\n   918→      .eq('id', order.id)\n   919→\n   920→    if (updateError) {\n   921→      console.error('Failed to update order status on dispute:', updateError)\n   922→      return\n   923→    }\n   924→\n   925→    console.log(`Order ${order.id} cancelled due to chargeback (dispute ${dispute.id}), fulfillment paused`)\n   926→\n   927→    // Create audit log entry\n   928→    await supabase\n   929→      .from('audit_log')\n   930→      .insert({\n   931→        actor_type: 'webhook',\n   932→        actor_id: 'stripe_webhook',\n   933→        action: 'order_disputed',\n   934→        resource_type: 'order',\n   935→        resource_id: order.id,\n   936→        changes: {\n   937→          status: 'disputed',\n   938→          dispute_id: dispute.id,\n   939→          dispute_reason: dispute.reason,\n   940→        },\n   941→        metadata: {\n   942→          payment_intent_id: paymentIntentId,\n   943→          dispute_amount: dispute.amount,\n   944→          dispute_currency: dispute.currency,\n   945→        },\n   946→      })\n   947→\n   948→    // Send admin alert notification\n   949→    const { data: admins } = await supabase\n   950→      .from('users')\n   951→      .select('id')\n   952→      .eq('role', 'admin')\n   953→\n   954→    if (admins && admins.length > 0) {\n   955→      const notifications = admins.map(admin => ({\n   956→        user_id: admin.id,\n   957→        type: 'chargeback',\n   958→        title: 'Chargeback Alert',\n   959→        body: `Order ${order.id.slice(0, 8)} has a chargeback (${dispute.reason}). Amount: ${(dispute.amount / 100).toFixed(2)} ${dispute.currency.toUpperCase()}. Fulfillment paused.`,\n   960→        data: {\n   961→          order_id: order.id,\n   962→          dispute_id: dispute.id,\n   963→          dispute_reason: dispute.reason,\n   964→          dispute_amount: dispute.amount,\n   965→          payment_intent_id: paymentIntentId,\n   966→        },\n   967→        is_read: false,\n   968→      }))\n   969→\n   970→      const { error: notifError } = await supabase\n   971→        .from('notifications')\n   972→        .insert(notifications)\n   973→\n   974→      if (notifError) {\n   975→        console.error('Failed to create admin chargeback notifications:', notifError)\n   976→      } else {\n   977→        console.log(`Created ${notifications.length} admin chargeback alerts for order ${order.id}`)\n   978→      }\n   979→    }\n   980→\n   981→    // Alert admin via API endpoint (fallback)\n   982→    fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/admin/alert`, {\n   983→      method: 'POST',\n   984→      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n   985→      body: JSON.stringify({\n   986→        type: 'chargeback',\n   987→        message: `Chargeback on order ${order.id.slice(0, 8)}: ${dispute.reason}`,\n   988→        severity: 'high',\n   989→        metadata: {\n   990→          order_id: order.id,\n   991→          dispute_id: dispute.id,\n   992→          amount: dispute.amount,\n   993→        },\n   994→      }),\n   995→    }).catch(() => {})\n   996→\n   997→    console.log(`Successfully processed chargeback for order ${order.id}`)\n   998→  } catch (error) {\n   999→    console.error('Error handling charge dispute created:', error)\n  1000→    // Don't throw - we don't want to cause Stripe to retry indefinitely\n  1001→  }\n  1002→}\n  1003→"}]},"uuid":"ea19484e-b64c-4248-bea9-984d2e5c5f3a","timestamp":"2026-03-07T18:38:20.805Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"93b7bf14-cdaf-4396-baf2-33ba8c8b2392"}

    1→/**\n     2→ * order.shipped handler — Parses shipment data from the normalized event,\n     3→ * updates the order record with tracking info, sends email notification,\n     4→ * and creates an in-app notification.\n     5→ *\n     6→ * Logic extracted from webhooks/printify/route.ts handleOrderShipped().\n     7→ */\n     8→\n     9→import type { NormalizedWebhookEvent } from '../../models'\n    10→import type { SupabaseClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\n    11→import { sendOrderShippedEmail } from '@/lib/resend'\n    12→import { findOrder, isEmailEnabled } from './utils'\n    13→\n    14→interface ShipmentData {\n    15→  carrier: string\n    16→  number: string\n    17→  url: string\n    18→}\n    19→\n    20→/**\n    21→ * Extract shipments array from normalized event data.\n    22→ * Printify: data.shipments[]\n    23→ * Printful: data.shipment (single) or data.order.shipments[]\n    24→ */\n    25→function extractShipments(data: Record<string, unknown>): ShipmentData[] {\n    26→  // Direct shipments array (Printify format)\n    27→  if (Array.isArray(data.shipments)) {\n    28→    return data.shipments as ShipmentData[]\n    29→  }\n    30→\n    31→  // Printful format: single shipment object\n    32→  if (data.shipment && typeof data.shipment === 'object') {\n    33→    const s = data.shipment as Record<string, unknown>\n    34→    return [{\n    35→      carrier: String(s.carrier || s.service || ''),\n    36→      number: String(s.tracking_number || s.number || ''),\n    37→      url: String(s.tracking_url || s.url || ''),\n    38→    }]\n    39→  }\n    40→\n    41→  // Printful nested format: data.order.shipments[]\n    42→  const orderObj = data.order as Record<string, unknown> | undefined\n    43→  if (orderObj && Array.isArray(orderObj.shipments)) {\n    44→    return (orderObj.shipments as Array<Record<string, unknown>>).map(s => ({\n    45→      carrier: String(s.carrier || s.service || ''),\n    46→      number: String(s.tracking_number || s.number || ''),\n    47→      url: String(s.tracking_url || s.url || ''),\n    48→    }))\n    49→  }\n    50→\n    51→  return []\n    52→}\n    53→\n    54→export async function handleOrderShipped(\n    55→  event: NormalizedWebhookEvent,\n    56→  supabase: SupabaseClient,\n    57→): Promise<void> {\n    58→  const order = await findOrder(event, supabase)\n    59→\n    60→  if (!order) {\n    61→    console.error(`[webhook:order.shipped] Order not found (provider: ${event.provider}, resourceId: ${event.resourceId})`)\n    62→    throw new Error('Order not found')\n    63→  }\n    64→\n    65→  const shipments = extractShipments(event.data)\n    66→\n    67→  // Build update payload\n    68→  const updateData: Record<string, unknown> = {\n    69→    status: 'shipped',\n    70→    shipped_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n    71→  }\n    72→\n    73→  if (shipments.length === 1) {\n    74→    updateData.tracking_number = shipments[0].number\n    75→    updateData.tracking_url = shipments[0].url\n    76→    updateData.carrier = shipments[0].carrier\n    77→  } else if (shipments.length > 1) {\n    78→    // Multiple shipments: concatenate tracking numbers, dedupe carriers\n    79→    updateData.tracking_number = shipments.map(s => s.number).join(', ')\n    80→    updateData.tracking_url = shipments[0].url\n    81→    updateData.carrier = shipments\n    82→      .map(s => s.carrier)\n    83→      .filter((v, i, a) => a.indexOf(v) === i)\n    84→      .join(', ')\n    85→  }\n    86→\n    87→  const primaryShipment = shipments[0] || null\n    88→\n    89→  // Update order status\n    90→  const { error } = await supabase\n    91→    .from('orders')\n    92→    .update(updateData)\n    93→    .eq('id', order.id)\n    94→\n    95→  if (error) {\n    96→    console.error('[webhook:order.shipped] Failed to update order:', error)\n    97→    throw error\n    98→  }\n    99→\n   100→  console.log(`[webhook:order.shipped] Order ${order.id} marked as shipped (provider: ${event.provider})`)\n   101→\n   102→  const orderDisplayId = order.id.slice(0, 8)\n   103→\n   104→  // Create in-app notification\n   105→  if (order.user_id) {\n   106→    const { error: notificationError } = await supabase.from('notifications').insert({\n   107→      user_id: order.user_id,\n   108→      type: 'order_shipped',\n   109→      title: `Order #${orderDisplayId} Shipped`,\n   110→      body: primaryShipment\n   111→        ? `Your order has been shipped via ${primaryShipment.carrier}. Tracking: ${primaryShipment.number}`\n   112→        : 'Your order has been shipped and is on its way!',\n   113→      data: {\n   114→        order_id: order.id,\n   115→        tracking_number: primaryShipment?.number,\n   116→        tracking_url: primaryShipment?.url,\n   117→        carrier: primaryShipment?.carrier,\n   118→      },\n   119→      is_read: false,\n   120→    })\n   121→\n   122→    if (notificationError) {\n   123→      console.error('[webhook:order.shipped] Failed to create notification:', notificationError)\n   124→    } else {\n   125→      console.log(`[webhook:order.shipped] Created notification for user ${order.user_id}`)\n   126→    }\n   127→  }\n   128→\n   129→  // Send email notification (respecting user preferences)\n   130→  if (order.customer_email && order.user_id) {\n   131→    const emailEnabled = await isEmailEnabled(order.user_id, supabase)\n   132→\n   133→    if (emailEnabled) {\n   134→      const emailResult = await sendOrderShippedEmail({\n   135→        to: order.customer_email,\n   136→        orderId: orderDisplayId,\n   137→        trackingNumber: primaryShipment?.number,\n   138→        trackingUrl: primaryShipment?.url,\n   139→        carrier: primaryShipment?.carrier,\n   140→        locale: order.locale || 'en',\n   141→      })\n   142→\n   143→      if (emailResult.success) {\n   144→        console.log(`[webhook:order.shipped] Email sent for order ${order.id}`)\n   145→      } else {\n   146→        console.error('[webhook:order.shipped] Failed to send email:', emailResult.error)\n   147→      }\n   148→    } else {\n   149→      console.log(`[webhook:order.shipped] Email skipped (user preference disabled)`)\n   150→    }\n   151→  }\n   152→\n   153→  // Audit log\n   154→  await supabase.from('audit_log').insert({\n   155→    actor_type: 'webhook',\n   156→    actor_id: `${event.provider}_webhook`,\n   157→    action: 'order_shipped',\n   158→    resource_type: 'order',\n   159→    resource_id: order.id,\n   160→    changes: updateData,\n   161→    metadata: {\n   162→      provider: event.provider,\n   163→      provider_order_id: event.resourceId,\n   164→    },\n   165→  })\n   166→}\n   167→"}]},"uuid":"c24a0f62-e772-4102-8eae-d96fdf184e7f","timestamp":"2026-03-07T18:38:20.773Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2da8709e-b301-431e-8ee6-d66498b508b6"}

 },\n    52→    // Product images from placeholder service (via.placeholder.com)\n    53→    {\n    54→      matcher: /^https:\\/\\/via\\.placeholder\\.com\\/.*/i,\n    55→      handler: new ],\n    67→      }),\n    68→    },\n    69→    // Supabase storage images\n    70→    {\n    71→      matcher: /^https:\\/\\/.*\\.supabase\\.co\\/storage\\/.*/i,\n    72→      handler: new CacheFirst({\n    73→        cacheName: 'supabase-images',\n    74→        plugins: [\n    75→          {\n    76→            cacheWillUpdate: async ({ response }) => {\n    77→              if 90→        cacheName: 'fal-images',\n    91→        plugins: [\n    92→          {\n    93→            cacheWillUpdate: async ({ response }) => {\n    94→              if (response && response.status === 200) {\n    95→                return response\n    96→              }\n    97→              return null\n    98→            },\n    99→          },\n   100→          {\n   101→            cacheDidUpdate: async () => {\n   102→              // Limit cache to 100 entries (design images can be large)\n   103→              const cache = await caches.open('fal-images')\n   104→              const keys = await cache.keys()\n   105→              if (keys.length > 100) {\n   106→                await cache.delete(keys[0])\n   107→              }\n   108→            },\n   109→          },\n   110→        ],\n   111→      }),\n   112→    },\n   113→  ],\n   114→  fallbacks: {\n   115→    entries: [\n   116→      {\n   117→        url: '/en/offline',\n   118→        matcher({ request }) {\n   119→          return request.destination === 'document'\n   120→        },\n   121→      },\n   122→    ],\n   123→  },\n   124→})\n   125→\n   126→serwist.addEventListeners()\n   127→\n   128→// Clean up old cache names on activation (printify-images → printify-images-v2)\n   129→self.addEventListener('activate', (event: any) => {\n   130→  event.waitUntil(\n   131→    caches.keys().then((names: string[]) =>\n   132→      Promise.all(\n   133→        names\n   134→          .filter((n) => n === 'printify-images')\n   135→          .map((n) => caches.delete(n))\n   136→      )\n   137→    )\n   138→  )\n   139→})\n   140→\n   141→// --- Web Push Notification Handlers (preserved from original sw.js) ---\n   142→self.addEventListener('push', (event: any) => {\n   143→  const data = event.data ? event.data.json() : {}\n   144→  const options: any = {\n   145→    body: data.body || '',\n   146→    icon: '/icon-192.png',\n   147→    badge: '/icon-192.png',\n   148→    data: { url: data.url || '/' },\n   149→    tag: data.tag || 'pod-ai-notification',\n   150→    actions: data.actions || [],\n   151→    vibrate: [100, 50, 100],\n   152→  }\n   153→  event.waitUntil(\n   154→    self.registration.showNotification(data.title || 'SKAPARA', options)\n   155→  )\n   156→})\n   157→\n   158→self.addEventListener('notificationclick', (event: any) => {\n   159→  event.notification.close()\n   160→  const url = event.notification.data?.url || '/'\n   161→  event.waitUntil(\n   162→    self.clients.matchAll({ type: 'window', includeUncontrolled: true }).then((clients: any) => {\n   163→      for (const client of clients) {\n   164→        if (client.url.includes(self.location.origin) && 'focus' in client) {\n   165→          client.navigate(url)\n   166→          return client.focus()\n   167→        }\n   168→      }\n   169→      return self.clients.openWindow(url)\n   170→    })\n   171→  )\n   172→})\n   173→\n   174→// --- Background Sync for Pending Cart Actions ---\n   175→self.addEventListener('sync', (event: any) => {\n   176→  if (event.tag === 'sync-cart') {\n   177→    event.waitUntil(syncCartActions())\n   178→  }\n   179→})\n   180→\n   181→async function syncCartActions() {\n   182→  // Get pending cart actions from IndexedDB\n   183→  const pendingActions = await getPendingCartActions()\n   184→\n   185→  for (const action of pendingActions) {\n   186→    try {\n   187→      // Replay the action\n   188→      const response = await fetch(action.url, {\n   189→        method: action.method,\n   190→        headers: action.headers,\n   191→        body: action.body,\n   192→      })\n   193→\n   194→      if (response.ok) {\n   195→        // Action succeeded, remove from pending queue\n   196→        await removePendingAction(action.id)\n   197→\n   198→        // Notify all clients that sync succeeded\n   199→        const clients = await self.clients.matchAll()\n   200→        clients.forEach((client: any) => {\n   201→          client.postMessage({\n   202→            type: 'sync-success',\n   203→            action: action.type,\n   204→          })\n   205→        })\n   206→      }\n   207→    } catch (error) {\n   208→      console.error('Failed to sync action:', action, error)\n   209→      // Will retry on next sync\n   210→    }\n   211→  }\n   212→}\n   213→\n   214→async function getPendingCartActions(): Promise<any[]> {\n   215→  try {\n   216→    const db = await openDB()\n   217→    const tx = db.transaction('pendingActions', 'readonly')\n   218→    const store = tx.objectStore('pendingActions')\n   219→    return await store.getAll()\n   220→  } catch {\n   221→    return []\n   222→  }\n   223→}\n   224→\n   225→async function removePendingAction(id: string) {\n   226→  try {\n   227→    const db = await openDB()\n   228→    const tx = db.transaction('pendingActions', 'readwrite')\n   229→    const store = tx.objectStore('pendingActions')\n   230→    await store.delete(id)\n   231→  } catch (error) {\n   232→    console.error('Failed to remove pending action:', error)\n   233→  }\n   234→}\n   235→\n   236→function openDB(): Promise<any> {\n   237→  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n   238→    const request = indexedDB.open('pod-ai-sync', 1)\n   239→\n   240→    request.onerror = () => reject(request.error)\n   241→    request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result)\n   242→\n   243→    request.onupgradeneeded = (event: any) => {\n   244→      const db = event.target.result\n   245→      if {"parentUuid":"d2fd3297-5f7e-49ca-b5f3-4d3cafc9b4c3","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project","sessionId":"e8096e0d-c27c-4885-8649-a1485c3b2f75","version":"2.1.71","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"a0820ca183c218d7b","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01R3xJAZtkmcLfWBkQtdgFHa","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→/**\n     2→ * Email Drip Sequence System\n     3→ *\n     4→ * Schedules a series of timed emails for new users.\n     5→ * Uses drip_queue table with send_at timestamps.\n     6→ * Processed by /api/cron/drip endpoint.\n     7→ */\n     8→\n     9→import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\n    10→\n    11→const supabase = createClient(\n    12→  process.env.SUPABASE_URL || process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n    13→  process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY!,\n    14→  { auth: { autoRefreshToken: false, persistSession: false } }\n    15→)\n    16→\n    17→export interface DripStep {\n    18→  delay_hours: number\n    19→  template: string\n    20→  subject: string\n    21→}\n    22→\n    23→export const DRIP_SEQUENCES: Record<string, DripStep[]> = {\n    24→  welcome: [\n    25→    { delay_hours: 1, template: 'welcome', subject: 'Welcome to Skapara — Your AI Design Studio' },\n    26→    { delay_hours: 72, template: 'tips', subject: '3 Ways to Create Amazing Designs with AI' },\n    27→    { delay_hours: 168, template: 'credit_offer', subject: 'Unlock More Designs — Upgrade to Premium' },\n    28→  ],\n    29→}\n    30→\n    31→/**\n    32→ * Schedule a drip sequence for a user.\n    33→ * Inserts rows into drip_queue with calculated send_at times.\n    34→ */\n    35→export async function triggerDripSequence(\n    36→  userId: string,\n    37→  email: string,\n    38→  sequence: string\n    39→): Promise<void> {\n    40→  const steps = DRIP_SEQUENCES[sequence]\n    41→  if (!steps) {\n    42→    console.warn(`[EmailDrip] Unknown sequence: ${sequence}`)\n    43→    return\n    44→  }\n    45→\n    46→  // Check if user already has this sequence queued\n    47→  const { data: existing } = await supabase\n    48→    .from('drip_queue')\n    49→    .select('id')\n    50→    .eq('user_id', userId)\n    51→    .eq('sequence', sequence)\n    52→    .limit(1)\n    53→\n    54→  if (existing && existing.length > 0) {\n    55→    return // Already queued\n    56→  }\n    57→\n    58→  const now = new Date()\n    59→  const rows = steps.map((step, index) => {\n    60→    const sendAt = new Date(now.getTime() + step.delay_hours * 60 * 60 * 1000)\n    61→    return {\n    62→      user_id: userId,\n    63→      email,\n    64→      sequence,\n    65→      step: index,\n    66→      template: step.template,\n    67→      subject: step.subject,\n    68→      send_at: sendAt.toISOString(),\n    69→      status: 'pending',\n    70→    }\n    71→  })\n    72→\n    73→  const { error } = await supabase.from('drip_queue').insert(rows)\n    74→  if (error) {\n    75→    console.error('[EmailDrip] Failed to queue sequence:', error)\n    76→  }\n    77→}\n    78→"}]},"uuid":"e8068e3a-c622-473c-bee5-14ad487282d3","timestamp":"2026-03-07T18:38:52.926Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d2fd3297-5f7e-49ca-b5f3-4d3cafc9b4c3"}

    1→/**\n     2→ * POST /api/newsletter/subscribe\n     3→ *\n     4→ * Newsletter subscription with double opt-in (GDPR/UWG compliant).\n     5→ * Creates unconfirmed subscriber and sends confirmation email.\n     6→ */\n     7→\n     8→import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\n     9→import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\n    10→import { z } from 'zod'\n    11→import crypto from 'crypto'\n    12→import { BASE_URL } from '@/lib/store-config'\n    13→\n    14→const supabase = createClient(\n    15→  process.env.SUPABASE_URL || process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n    16→  process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY!,\n    17→  { auth: { autoRefreshToken: false, persistSession: false } }\n    18→)\n    19→\n    20→const subscribeSchema = z.object({\n    21→  email: z.string().email(),\n    22→  locale: z.enum(['en', 'es', 'de']).default('en'),\n    23→})\n    24→\n    25→export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n    26→  try {\n    27→    const body = await req.json()\n    28→    const parsed = subscribeSchema.safeParse(body)\n    29→\n    30→    if (!parsed.success) {\n    31→      return NextResponse.json(\n    32→        { error: 'Invalid email or locale' },\n    33→        { status: 400 }\n    34→      )\n    35→    }\n    36→\n    37→    const { email, locale } = parsed.data\n    38→\n    39→    // Generate cryptographically secure confirmation token\n    40→    const confirmationToken = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex')\n    41→\n    42→    // Check if subscriber already exists\n    43→    const { data: existing } = await supabase\n    44→      .from('newsletter_subscribers')\n    45→      .select('id, confirmed_at')\n    46→      .eq('email', email)\n    47→      .single()\n    48→\n    49→    if (existing) {\n    50→      if (existing.confirmed_at) {\n    51→        // Already confirmed - just return success (don't reveal this info)\n    52→        return NextResponse.json({ message: 'Confirmation email sent' })\n    53→      } else {\n    54→        // Update existing unconfirmed subscriber with new token\n    55→        await supabase\n    56→          .from('newsletter_subscribers')\n    57→          .update({\n    58→            confirmation_token: confirmationToken,\n    59→            locale,\n    60→          })\n    61→          .eq('email', email)\n    62→      }\n    63→    } else {\n    64→      // Create new subscriber (unconfirmed)\n    65→      const { error: insertError } = await supabase\n    66→        .from('newsletter_subscribers')\n    67→        .insert({\n    68→          email,\n    69→          locale,\n    70→          confirmation_token: confirmationToken,\n    71→          subscribed: true,\n    72→        })\n    73→\n    74→      if (insertError) {\n    75→        console.error('[Newsletter] Insert error:', insertError)\n    76→        return NextResponse.json(\n    77→          { error: 'Failed to create subscription' },\n    78→          { status: 500 }\n    79→        )\n    80→      }\n    81→    }\n    82→\n    83→    // Send confirmation email via Resend\n    84→    const confirmUrl = `${BASE_URL}/api/newsletter/confirm/${confirmationToken}`\n    85→\n    86→    const resendKey = process.env.RESEND_API_KEY\n    87→    if (!resendKey) {\n    88→      console.error('[Newsletter] RESEND_API_KEY not configured')\n    89→      return NextResponse.json(\n    90→        { error: 'Email service not configured' },\n    91→        { status: 500 }\n    92→      )\n    93→    }\n    94→\n    95→    // Localized confirmation email content\n    96→    const emailContent = {\n    97→      en: {\n    98→        subject: 'Confirm your newsletter subscription',\n    99→        body: `\n   100→          <h1>Confirm Your Subscription</h1>\n   101→          <p>Thanks for subscribing to Skapara newsletter!</p>\n   102→          <p>Please confirm your email address by clicking the link below:</p>\n   103→          <p><a href=\"${confirmUrl}\" style=\"display: inline-block; padding: 12px 24px; background: #000; color: #fff; text-decoration: none; border-radius: 6px;\">Confirm Subscription</a></p>\n   104→          <p>Or copy and paste this link into your browser:</p>\n   105→          <p style=\"font-size: 12px; color: #666;\">${confirmUrl}</p>\n   106→          <hr style=\"margin: 30px 0; border: none; border-top: 1px solid #eee;\">\n   107→          <p style=\"font-size: 12px; color: #999;\">\n   108→            If you didn't request this, you can safely ignore this email.\n   109→          </p>\n   110→        `,\n   111→      },\n   112→      es: {\n   113→        subject: 'Confirma tu suscripción al boletín',\n   114→        body: `\n   115→          <h1>Confirma tu suscripción</h1>\n   116→          <p>¡Gracias por suscribirte al boletín de Skapara!</p>\n   117→          <p>Por favor confirma tu dirección de correo electrónico haciendo clic en el siguiente enlace:</p>\n   118→          <p><a href=\"${confirmUrl}\" style=\"display: inline-block; padding: 12px 24px; background: #000; color: #fff; text-decoration: none; border-radius: 6px;\">Confirmar suscripción</a></p>\n   119→          <p>O copia y pega este enlace en tu navegador:</p>\n   120→          <p style=\"font-size: 12px; color: #666;\">${confirmUrl}</p>\n   121→          <hr style=\"margin: 30px 0; border: none; border-top: 1px solid #eee;\">\n   122→          <p style=\"font-size: 12px; color: #999;\">\n   123→            Si no solicitaste esto, puedes ignorar este correo de forma segura.\n   124→          </p>\n   125→        `,\n   126→      },\n   127→      de: {\n   128→        subject: 'Bestätigen Sie Ihr Newsletter-Abonnement',\n   129→        body: `\n   130→          <h1>Bestätigen Sie Ihr Abonnement</h1>\n   131→          <p>Vielen Dank für Ihr Abonnement des Skapara Newsletters!</p>\n   132→          <p>Bitte bestätigen Sie Ihre E-Mail-Adresse, indem Sie auf den folgenden Link klicken:</p>\n   133→          <p><a href=\"${confirmUrl}\" style=\"display: inline-block; padding: 12px 24px; background: #000; color: #fff; text-decoration: none; border-radius: 6px;\">Abonnement bestätigen</a></p>\n   134→          <p>Oder kopieren Sie diesen Link in Ihren Browser:</p>\n   135→          <p style=\"font-size: 12px; color: #666;\">${confirmUrl}</p>\n   136→          <hr style=\"margin: 30px 0; border: none; border-top: 1px solid #eee;\">\n   137→          <p style=\"font-size: 12px; color: #999;\">\n   138→            Wenn Sie dies nicht angefordert haben, können Sie diese E-Mail ignorieren.\n   139→          </p>\n   140→        `,\n   141→      },\n   142→    }\n   143→\n   144→    const content = emailContent[locale]\n   145→\n   146→    const res = await fetch('https://api.resend.com/emails', {\n   147→      method: 'POST',\n   148→      headers: {\n   149→        'Authorization': `Bearer ${resendKey}`,\n   150→        'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n   151→      },\n   152→      body: JSON.stringify({\n   153→        from: process.env.RESEND_FROM_EMAIL || 'SKAPARA <noreply@skapara.com>',\n   154→        to: email,\n   155→        subject: content.subject,\n   156→        html: content.body,\n   157→      }),\n   158→    })\n   159→\n   160→    if (!res.ok) {\n   161→      const errorText = await res.text()\n   162→      console.error('[Newsletter] Resend error:', errorText)\n   163→      return NextResponse.json(\n   164→        { error: 'Failed to send confirmation email' },\n   165→        { status: 500 }\n   166→      )\n   167→    }\n   168→\n   169→    return NextResponse.json({ message: 'Confirmation email sent' })\n   170→  } catch (error) {\n   171→    console.error('[Newsletter] Subscribe error:', error)\n   172→    return NextResponse.json(\n   173→      { error: 'Subscription failed' },\n   174→      { status: 500 }\n   175→    )\n   176→  }\n   177→}\n   178→"}]},"uuid":"c6463b87-b48d-4c14-8d34-ad505410f613","timestamp":"2026-03-07T18:38:52.979Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"45b727c0-99b7-4f75-8845-451fe789e7a0"}

Please try again later.' },\n    25→      {\n    26→        status: 429,\n    27→        headers: {\n    28→          'X-RateLimit-Limit': '10',\n    29→          'X-RateLimit-Remaining': String(rateLimitResult.remaining),\n    30→          'Retry-After': '60',\n    31→        }\n    32→      }\n    33→    )\n    34→  }\n    35→\n    36→  try {\n    37→    const body = await request.json();\n    38→    const { email } = body;\n    39→\n    40→    if (!email || typeof email !== 'string') {\n    41→      return NextResponse.json(\n    42→        { error: 'Email is required' },\n    43→        { status: 400 }\n    44→      );\n    45→    }\n    46→\n    47→    const supabase = supabaseAdmin;\n    48→\n    49→    // Check if user exists\n    50→    const { data: user, error: fetchError } = await supabase\n    51→      .from('users')\n    52→      .select('id, email, notification_preferences')\n    53→      .eq('email', email)\n    54→      .single();\n    55→\n    56→    if (fetchError || !user) {\n    57→      // Even if user doesn't exist, confirm unsubscribe for privacy\n    58→      return NextResponse.json({\n    59→        success: true,\n    60→        message: 'You have been unsubscribed from all marketing emails.',\n    61→        email,\n    62→      });\n    63→    }\n    64→\n    65→    // Update user notification preferences to disable marketing emails\n    66→    const preferences = user.notification_preferences || {};\n    67→    const updatedPreferences = {\n    68→      ...preferences,\n    69→      marketing_emails: false,\n    70→      newsletter: false,\n    71→      unsubscribed_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n    72→    };\n    73→\n    74→    const { error: updateError } = await supabase\n    75→      .from('users')\n    76→      .update({\n    77→        notification_preferences: updatedPreferences,\n    78→      })\n    79→      .eq('id', user.id);\n    80→\n    81→    if (updateError) {\n    82→      console.error('Unsubscribe update error:', updateError);\n    83→      return NextResponse.json(\n    84→        { error: 'Failed to update preferences' },\n    85→        { status: 500 }\n    86→      );\n    87→    }\n    88→\n    89→    // Log the unsubscribe event\n    90→    await supabase.from('audit_log').insert({\n    91→      user_id: user.id,\n    92→      action: 'newsletter_unsubscribe',\n    93→      details: {\n    94→        email,\n    95→        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n    96→        method: 'api',\n    97→      },\n    98→    });\n    99→\n   100→    return NextResponse.json({\n   101→      success: true,\n   102→      message: 'You have been unsubscribed from all marketing emails.',\n   103→      email,\n   104→      honored_within: '24 hours',\n   105→      compliance: 'CAN-SPAM compliant',\n   106→    });\n   107→  } catch (error) {\n   108→    console.error('Unsubscribe API error:', error);\n   109→    return NextResponse.json(\n   110→      { error: 'Internal server error', details: String(error) },\n   111→      { status: 500 }\n   112→    );\n   113→  }\n   114→}\n   115→\n   116→/**\n   117→ * Handle one-click unsubscribe via token (RFC 8058) or check status via email\n   118→ * GET /api/newsletter/unsubscribe?token=X - One-click unsubscribe\n   119→ * GET /api/newsletter/unsubscribe?email=X - Check unsubscribe status\n   120→ */\n   121→export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {\n   122→  // Rate limiting: 10 requests per minute per IP\n   123→  const clientIP = isTokenBased = false;\n   146→\n   147→    // Token-based unsubscribe (one-click, RFC 8058)\n   148→    if (token) {\n   149→      const decoded = verifyUnsubscribeToken(token);\n   150→      if (!decoded) {\n   151→        return NextResponse.json(\n   152→          { error: 'Invalid or expired unsubscribe token' },\n   153→          { status: 400 }\n   154→        );\n   155→      }\n hours',\n   226→        compliance: 'RFC 8058 + CAN-SPAM compliant',\n   227→      });\n   228→    }\n   229→\n   230→    // Email-based status check\n   231→    const { data: user, error } = await supabase\n   232→      .from('users')\n   233→      .select('email, notification_preferences')\n   234→      .eq('email', email)\n   235→      .single();\n   236→\n   237→    if (error || !user) {\n
